<FILE_SYSTEM_GUIDELINES>

</FILE_SYSTEM_GUIDELINES>

    70	
    71	What is NiBetaSeries?
    72	---------------------
    73	NiBetaSeries is BIDS_-compatible `application <https://bids-apps.neuroimaging.io/>`_
    74	that calculates betaseries correlations.
    75	In brief, a beta coefficient (i.e., parameter estimate) is calculated
    76	for each trial (or event) resulting in a series of betas ("betaseries")
    77	that can be correlated across regions of interest.
    78	
    79	Why should I use it?
    80	--------------------
    81	There are potential insights hidden in your task fMRI data.
    82	Rest fMRI enjoys a multitude of toolboxes which can be applied to task fMRI
    83	with some effort, but there are not many toolboxes that focus on making
    84	betaseries.
    85	Betaseries can then be used for correlations/classifications and
    86	a multitude of other analyses.
    87	While a couple alternatives exist (pybetaseries_ and BASCO_), NiBetaSeries
    88	is the only application to interface with BIDS_ organized data with the goal
    89	of providing a command-line application experience like fMRIPrep_.
    90	
    91	What does NiBetaSeries give me?
    92	-------------------------------
    93	Currently NiBetaSeries returns the beta series images and optionally
    94	symmetric z-transformed correlation matrices with an entry for each
    95	parcel defined in the atlas.
    96	
    97	.. note:: The betas (i.e., parameter estimates) are generated using either
    98	    the "Least Squares Separate" or "Least Squares All" procedures.
    99	    Please read the :ref:`betaseries` page for more background information.
   100	
   101	What do I need to run NiBetaSeries?
   102	-----------------------------------
   103	NiBetaSeries takes BIDS_ and preprocessed data as input that satisfy the
   104	`BIDS derivatives specification <http://bit.ly/2vKeKcp>`_.
   105	In practical terms, NiBetaSeries uses the output of fMRIPrep_,
   106	a great BIDS-compatible preprocessing tool.
   107	NiBetaSeries requires the input and the atlas to already
   108	be in the same space (e.g., MNI space).
   109	For more details, see :ref:`usage` and the tutorial
   110	(:ref:`sphx_glr_auto_examples_plot_run_nibetaseries.py`)
   111	
   112	Get Involved
   113	------------
   114	This is a very young project that still needs some tender loving care to grow.
   115	That's where you fit in!
   116	If you would like to contribute, please read our :ref:`code_of_conduct`
   117	and contributing page (:ref:`contributing`).
   118	
   119	Thanks!
   120	-------
   121	This project heavily leverages `nipype <http://nipype.readthedocs.io/en/latest/>`_,
   122	`nilearn <https://nilearn.github.io/>`_, `pybids <https://bids-standard.github.io/pybids/>`_, and
   123	`nistats <https://nistats.github.io/>`_ for development.
   124	Please check out their pages and support the developers.
   125	
   126	.. _BASCO: https://www.nitrc.org/projects/basco/
   127	.. _pybetaseries: https://github.com/poldrack/pybetaseries
   128	.. _BIDS: http://bids.neuroimaging.io/
   129	.. _fMRIPrep: http://fmriprep.readthedocs.io/en/latest/
   130

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python
     2	# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
     3	# emacs: -*- mode: python; py-indent-offset: 4; indent-tabs-mode: nil -*-
     4	# vi: set ft=python sts=4 ts=4 sw=4 et:
     5	from nipype.interfaces.base import (
     6	    BaseInterfaceInputSpec, TraitedSpec,
     7	    OutputMultiPath, File, LibraryBaseInterface,
     8	    SimpleInterface, traits
     9	    )
    10	
    11	
    12	class NistatsBaseInterface(LibraryBaseInterface):
    13	    _pkg = 'nistats'
    14	
    15	
    16	class LSSBetaSeriesInputSpec(BaseInterfaceInputSpec):
    17	    bold_file = File(exists=True, mandatory=True,
    18	                     desc="The bold run")
    19	    bold_metadata = traits.Dict(desc='Dictionary containing useful information about'
    20	                                ' the bold_file')
    21	    mask_file = File(exists=True, mandatory=True,
    22	                     desc="Binarized nifti file indicating the brain")
    23	    events_file = File(exists=True, mandatory=True,
    24	                       desc="File that contains all events from the bold run")
    25	    confounds_file = traits.Either(None, File(exists=True),
    26	                                   desc="File that contains all usable confounds")
    27	    selected_confounds = traits.Either(None, traits.List(),
    28	                                       desc="Column names of the regressors to include")
    29	    hrf_model = traits.String(desc="hemodynamic response model")
    30	    smoothing_kernel = traits.Either(None, traits.Float(),
    31	                                     desc="full wide half max smoothing kernel")
    32	    high_pass = traits.Float(0.0078125, desc="the high pass filter (Hz)")
    33	    fir_delays = traits.Either(None, traits.List(traits.Int),
    34	                               desc="FIR delays (in scans)")
    35	
    36	
    37	class LSSBetaSeriesOutputSpec(TraitedSpec):
    38	    beta_maps = OutputMultiPath(File)
    39	    design_matrices = traits.Dict()
    40	
    41	
    42	class LSSBetaSeries(NistatsBaseInterface, SimpleInterface):
    43	    """Calculates BetaSeries Maps From a BOLD file (one series map per event type)."""
    44	    input_spec = LSSBetaSeriesInputSpec
    45	    output_spec = LSSBetaSeriesOutputSpec
    46	
    47	    def _run_interface(self, runtime):
    48	        from nistats import first_level_model
    49	        import nibabel as nib
    50	        import os
    51	
    52	        # get t_r from bold_metadata
    53	        t_r = self.inputs.bold_metadata['RepetitionTime']
    54	
    55	        # get the confounds:
    56	        if self.inputs.confounds_file and self.inputs.selected_confounds:
    57	            confounds = _select_confounds(self.inputs.confounds_file,
    58	                                          self.inputs.selected_confounds)
    59	        else:
    60	            confounds = None
    61	
    62	        # setup the model
    63	        model = first_level_model.FirstLevelModel(
    64	            t_r=t_r,
    65	            slice_time_ref=0,
    66	            hrf_model=self.inputs.hrf_model,
    67	            mask=self.inputs.mask_file,
    68	            smoothing_fwhm=self.inputs.smoothing_kernel,
    69	            standardize=True,
    70	            signal_scaling=0,
    71	            high_pass=self.inputs.high_pass,
    72	            drift_model='cosine',
    73	            verbose=1,
    74	            fir_delays=self.inputs.fir_delays,
    75	        )
    76	
    77	        # initialize dictionary to contain trial estimates (betas)
    78	        beta_maps = {}
    79	        design_matrix_collector = {}
    80	        for target_trial_df, trial_type, trial_idx in \
    81	                _lss_events_iterator(self.inputs.events_file):
    82	
    83	            # fit the model for the target trial
    84	            model.fit(self.inputs.bold_file,
    85	                      events=target_trial_df,
    86	                      confounds=confounds)
    87	
    88	            if self.inputs.hrf_model == 'fir':
    89	                # FS modeling
    90	                for delay in self.inputs.fir_delays:
    91	                    delay_ttype = trial_type+'_delay_{}'.format(delay)
    92	                    new_delay_ttype = delay_ttype.replace('_delay_{}'.format(delay),
    93	                                                          'Delay{}Vol'.format(delay))
    94	                    beta_map = model.compute_contrast(
    95	                        delay_ttype, output_type='effect_size')
    96	                    if new_delay_ttype in beta_maps:
    97	                        beta_maps[new_delay_ttype].append(beta_map)
    98	                    else:
    99	                        beta_maps[new_delay_ttype] = [beta_map]
   100	            else:
   101	                # calculate the beta map
   102	                beta_map = model.compute_contrast(trial_type, output_type='effect_size')
   103	                design_matrix_collector[trial_idx] = model.design_matrices_[0]
   104	                # assign beta map to appropriate list
   105	                if trial_type in beta_maps:
   106	                    beta_maps[trial_type].append(beta_map)
   107	                else:
   108	                    beta_maps[trial_type] = [beta_map]
   109	
   110	        # make a beta series from each beta map list
   111	        beta_series_template = os.path.join(runtime.cwd,
   112	                                            'desc-{trial_type}_betaseries.nii.gz')
   113	        # collector for the betaseries files
   114	        beta_series_lst = []
   115	        for t_type, betas in beta_maps.items():
   116	            beta_series = nib.funcs.concat_images(betas)
   117	            nib.save(beta_series, beta_series_template.format(trial_type=t_type))
   118	            beta_series_lst.append(beta_series_template.format(trial_type=t_type))
   119	
   120	        self._results['beta_maps'] = beta_series_lst
   121	        self._results['design_matrices'] = design_matrix_collector
   122	        return runtime
   123	
   124

   127	                     desc="The bold run")

   129	                                ' the bold_file')

   149	class LSABetaSeries(NistatsBaseInterface, SimpleInterface):
   150	    """Calculates BetaSeries Maps From a BOLD file (one series map per event type)."""
   151	    input_spec = LSABetaSeriesInputSpec
   152	    output_spec = LSABetaSeriesOutputSpec
   153	
   154	    def _run_interface(self, runtime):
   155	        from nistats import first_level_model
   156	        import nibabel as nib
   157	        import os
   158	
   159	        # get t_r from bold_metadata
   160	        t_r = self.inputs.bold_metadata['RepetitionTime']
   161

   164	            confounds = _select_confounds(self.inputs.confounds_file,
   165	                                          self.inputs.selected_confounds)
   166	        else:
   167	            confounds = None

   170	        model = first_level_model.FirstLevelModel(
   171	            t_r=t_r,
   172	            slice_time_ref=0,
   173	            hrf_model=self.inputs.hrf_model,
   174	            mask=self.inputs.mask_file,
   175	            smoothing_fwhm=self.inputs.smoothing_kernel,
   176	            standardize=True,

   182

   184	        beta_maps = {}
   185	        lsa_df = _lsa_events_converter(self.inputs.events_file)
   186	        model.fit(self.inputs.bold_file, events=lsa_df, confounds=confounds)
   187	        design_matrix = model.design_matrices_[0]
   188	        for i_trial in lsa_df.index:

   190	            t_type = lsa_df.loc[i_trial, 'original_trial_type']
   191	
   192	            # calculate the beta map
   193	            beta_map = model.compute_contrast(t_name, output_type='effect_size')
   194	
   195	            # assign beta map to appropriate list
   196	            if t_type in beta_maps:
   197	                beta_maps[t_type].append(beta_map)
   198	            else:
   199	                beta_maps[t_type] = [beta_map]
   200	
   201	        # make a beta series from each beta map list
   202	        beta_series_template = os.path.join(runtime.cwd,
   203	                                            'desc-{trial_type}_betaseries.nii.gz')
   204	        # collector for the betaseries files
   205	        beta_series_lst = []
   206	        for t_type, betas in beta_maps.items():
   207	            beta_series = nib.funcs.concat_images(betas)
   208	            nib.save(beta_series, beta_series_template.format(trial_type=t_type))
   209	            beta_series_lst.append(beta_series_template.format(trial_type=t_type))
   210	
   211	        self._results['beta_maps'] = beta_series_lst
   212	        self._results['design_matrices'] = [design_matrix]
   213	        return runtime
   214	
   215	
   216	def _lsa_events_converter(events_file):
   217	    """Make a model where each trial has its own regressor using least squares
   218	    all (LSA)
   219	
   220	    Parameters
   221	    ----------
   222	    events_file : str
   223	        File that contains all events from the bold run
   224	
   225	    Yields
   226	    ------
   227	    events : DataFrame
   228	        A DataFrame in which each trial has its own trial_type
   229	    """
   230	
   231	    import pandas as pd
   232	    events = pd.read_csv(events_file, sep='\t')
   233	    events['original_trial_type'] = events['trial_type']
   234	    for cond, cond_df in events.groupby('trial_type'):
   235	        cond_idx = cond_df.index
   236	        for i_trial, trial_idx in enumerate(cond_idx):
   237	            trial_name = '{0}_{1:04d}'.format(cond, i_trial+1)
   238	            events.loc[trial_idx, 'trial_type'] = trial_name
   239	    return events
   240	
   241	
   242	def _lss_events_iterator(events_file):
   243	    """Make a model for each trial using least squares separate (LSS)
   244	
   245	    Parameters
   246	    ----------
   247	    events_file : str
   248	        File that contains all events from the bold run
   249	
   250	    Yields
   251	    ------
   252	    events_trial : DataFrame
   253	        A DataFrame in which the target trial maintains its trial type,
   254	        but all other trials are assigned to 'other'
   255	    trial_type : str
   256	        The trial_type of the target trial
   257	    trial_counter : int
   258	        The marker for the nth trial of that type
   259	    """
   260	
   261	    import pandas as pd
   262	    import numpy as np
   263	    events = pd.read_csv(events_file, sep='\t')
   264	    trial_counter = dict([(t, 0) for t in np.unique(events['trial_type'])])
   265	    for trial_id in range(len(events)):
   266	        trial_type = events.loc[trial_id, 'trial_type']
   267	        # make a copy of the dataframe
   268	        events_trial = events.copy()
   269	        # assign new name to all events from original condition
   270	        trial_type_id = events_trial['trial_type'] == trial_type
   271	        events_trial.loc[trial_type_id, 'trial_type'] = 'other'
   272	        # assign the trial of interest to be its original value
   273	        events_trial.loc[trial_id, 'trial_type'] = trial_type
   274	        yield events_trial, trial_type, trial_counter[trial_type]
   275	        trial_counter[trial_type] += 1
   276	
   277	
   278	def _select_confounds(confounds_file, selected_confounds):
   279	    """Process and return selected confounds from the confounds file
   280	
   281	    Parameters
   282	    ----------
   283	    confounds_file : str
   284	        File that contains all usable confounds
   285	    selected_confounds : list
   286	        List containing all desired confounds.
   287	        confounds can be listed as regular expressions (e.g., "motion_outlier.*")
   288	
   289	    Returns
   290	    -------
   291	    desired_confounds : DataFrame
   292	        contains all desired (processed) confounds.
   293	    """
   294	    import pandas as pd
   295	    import numpy as np
   296	    import re
   297	
   298	    confounds_df = pd.read_csv(confounds_file, sep='\t', na_values='n/a')
   299	    # regular expression to capture confounds specified at the command line
   300	    confound_expr = re.compile(r"|".join(selected_confounds))
   301	    expanded_confounds = list(filter(confound_expr.fullmatch, confounds_df.columns))
   302	    imputables = ('framewise_displacement', 'std_dvars', 'dvars', '.*derivative1.*')
   303	
   304	    # regular expression to capture all imputable confounds
   305	    impute_expr = re.compile(r"|".join(imputables))
   306	    expanded_imputables = list(filter(impute_expr.fullmatch, expanded_confounds))
   307	    for imputable in expanded_imputables:
   308	        vals = confounds_df[imputable].values
   309	        if not np.isnan(vals[0]):
   310	            continue
   311	        # Impute the mean non-zero, non-NaN value
   312	        confounds_df[imputable][0] = np.nanmean(vals[vals != 0])
   313	
   314	    desired_confounds = confounds_df[expanded_confounds]
   315	    # check to see if there are any remaining nans
   316	    if desired_confounds.isna().values.any():
   317	        msg = "The selected confounds contain nans: {conf}".format(conf=expanded_confounds)
   318	        raise ValueError(msg)
   319	    return desired_confounds
   320

   204	def preproc_file(deriv_dir, sub_metadata, deriv_bold_fname=deriv_bold_fname):
   205	    deriv_bold = deriv_dir.ensure(deriv_bold_fname)
   206	    with open(str(sub_metadata), 'r') as md:
   207	        bold_metadata = json.load(md)
   208	    tr = bold_metadata["RepetitionTime"]
   209	    # time_points
   210	    tp = 200
   211	    ix = np.arange(tp)
   212	    # create voxel timeseries
   213	    task_onsets = np.zeros(tp)
   214	    # add activations at every 40 time points
   215	    # waffles
   216	    task_onsets[0::40] = 1
   217	    # fries
   218	    task_onsets[3::40] = 1.5
   219	    # milkshakes
   220	    task_onsets[6::40] = 2
   221	    signal = np.convolve(task_onsets, spm_hrf(tr))[0:len(task_onsets)]
   222	    # csf
   223	    csf = np.cos(2*np.pi*ix*(50/tp)) * 0.1
   224	    # white matter
   225	    wm = np.sin(2*np.pi*ix*(22/tp)) * 0.1
   226	    # voxel time series (signal and noise)
   227	    voxel_ts = signal + csf + wm
   228	    # a 4d matrix with 2 identical timeseries
   229	    img_data = np.array([[[voxel_ts, voxel_ts]]])
   230	    # make a nifti image
   231	    img = nib.Nifti1Image(img_data, np.eye(4))
   232	    # save the nifti image
   233	    img.to_filename(str(deriv_bold))
   234	
   235	    return deriv_bold
   236	
   237	
   238	@pytest.fixture(scope='session')
   239	def sub_events(bids_dir, sub_metadata, preproc_file,
   240	               bids_events_fname=bids_events_fname):
   241	    events_file = bids_dir.ensure(bids_events_fname)
   242	    # read in subject metadata to get the TR
   243	    with open(str(sub_metadata), 'r') as md:
   244	        bold_metadata = json.load(md)
   245	    tr = bold_metadata["RepetitionTime"]
   246	    # time_points
   247	    tp = nib.load(str(preproc_file)).shape[-1]
   248	    # create voxel timeseries
   249	    task_onsets = np.zeros(tp)
   250	    # add waffles at every 40 time points
   251	    task_onsets[0::40] = 1
   252	    # add fries at every 40 time points starting at 3
   253	    task_onsets[3::40] = 1
   254	    # add milkshakes at every 40 time points starting at 6
   255	    task_onsets[6::40] = 1
   256	    # create event tsv
   257	    num_trials = np.where(task_onsets == 1)[0].shape[0]
   258	    onsets = np.multiply(np.where(task_onsets == 1), tr).reshape(num_trials)
   259	    durations = [1] * num_trials
   260	    num_conds = 3
   261	    trial_types = ['waffle', 'fry', 'milkshake'] * int((num_trials / num_conds))
   262	    events_df = pd.DataFrame.from_dict({'onset': onsets,
   263	                                        'duration': durations,
   264	                                        'trial_type': trial_types})
   265	    # reorder columns
   266	    events_df = events_df[['onset', 'duration', 'trial_type']]
   267	    # save the events_df to file
   268	    events_df.to_csv(str(events_file), index=False, sep='\t')
   269	    return events_file
   270	
   271	
   272	@pytest.fixture(scope='session')
   273	def confounds_file(deriv_dir, preproc_file,
   274	                   deriv_regressor_fname=deriv_regressor_fname):
   275	    confounds_file = deriv_dir.ensure(deriv_regressor_fname)
   276	    confound_dict = {}
   277	    tp = nib.load(str(preproc_file)).shape[-1]
   278	    ix = np.arange(tp)
   279	    # csf
   280	    confound_dict['csf'] = np.cos(2*np.pi*ix*(50/tp)) * 0.1
   281	    # white matter
   282	    confound_dict['white_matter'] = np.sin(2*np.pi*ix*(22/tp)) * 0.1
   283	    # framewise_displacement
   284	    confound_dict['framewise_displacement'] = np.random.random_sample(tp)
   285	    confound_dict['framewise_displacement'][0] = np.nan
   286	    # motion outliers
   287	    for motion_outlier in range(0, 5):
   288	        mo_name = 'motion_outlier0{}'.format(motion_outlier)
   289	        confound_dict[mo_name] = np.zeros(tp)
   290	        confound_dict[mo_name][motion_outlier] = 1
   291	    # derivatives
   292	    derive1 = [
   293	        'csf_derivative1',
   294	        'csf_derivative1_power2',
   295	        'global_signal_derivative1_power2',
   296	        'trans_x_derivative1',
   297	        'trans_y_derivative1',
   298	        'trans_z_derivative1',
   299	        'trans_x_derivative1_power2',
   300	        'trans_y_derivative1_power2',
   301	        'trans_z_derivative1_power2',
   302	    ]
   303	    for d in derive1:
   304	        confound_dict[d] = np.random.random_sample(tp)
   305	        confound_dict[d][0] = np.nan
   306	
   307	    # transformations
   308	    for dir in ["trans_x", "trans_y", "trans_z"]:
   309	        confound_dict[dir] = np.random.random_sample(tp)
   310	
   311	    confounds_df = pd.DataFrame(confound_dict)
   312	    confounds_df.to_csv(str(confounds_file), index=False, sep='\t', na_rep='n/a')
   313	    return confounds_file

## Phase 3. EXPLORATION: Finding Related Files and Solutions<tool_call>think<arg_key>thought</arg_key><arg_value>Based on my analysis so far, I can see that:

1. The issue is about allowing users to pass nibabel objects directly to nistats interfaces instead of file paths
2. The main interfaces are in `/workspace/HBClab__NiBetaSeries__0.4/src/nibetaseries/interfaces/nistats.py`
3. There are two main classes: LSSBetaSeries and LSABetaSeries
4. Both have a `bold_file` input that currently expects a File path
5. The issue mentions "lss/lsa interfaces" which maps to these two classes

Looking at the code:
- Line 17: `bold_file = File(exists=True, mandatory=True, desc="The bold run")`
- Line 126: Same pattern for LSABetaSeries

The problem is that these inputs are defined as File types which expect file paths, not nibabel objects.

From the issue description, the user wants to avoid the bottleneck of reading/writing nifti files to disk. Currently, the interfaces:
1. Take a file path for bold_file
2. Pass that file path to nistats.model.FirstLevelModel.fit()
3. Which internally reads the file from disk

The solution should allow:
1. Passing either file paths (backward compatibility) OR nibabel objects
2. Internally handling both cases appropriately
3. Not breaking existing functionality

    13	class CensorVolumesInputSpec(BaseInterfaceInputSpec):
    14	    timeseries_file = File(exists=True, mandatory=True,
    15	                           desc="a 4d nifti file")
    16	    mask_file = File(exists=True, mandatory=True,
    17	                     desc='binary mask for the 4d nifti file')
    18	    threshold = traits.Float(default_value=10.0,
    19	                             usedefault=True,
    20	                             desc="the modified z-score to use as a threshold")

    23	class CensorVolumesOutputSpec(TraitedSpec):
    24	    censored_file = File(exists=True,
    25	                         desc="a 4d nifti file with extreme volumes removed")
    26	    outliers = traits.Array(desc="boolean array indicating which indices are noise")

    29	class CensorVolumes(SimpleInterface):
    30	    """remove volumes with extremely large outliers"""
    31	
    32	    input_spec = CensorVolumesInputSpec
    33	    output_spec = CensorVolumesOutputSpec

    35	    def _run_interface(self, runtime):
    36	        import nibabel as nib
    37	        from nipype.utils.filemanip import fname_presuffix
    38	
    39	        bold_img = nib.load(self.inputs.timeseries_file)

    42	        bold_data = bold_img.get_fdata()
    43	        bold_mask = bold_mask_img.get_fdata().astype(bool)

    50	                           bold_img.affine, bold_img.header).to_filename(out)
    51

    53	        self._results['outliers'] = outliers
    54	
    55	        return runtime

    67	class AtlasConnectivityOutputSpec(TraitedSpec):
    68	    correlation_matrix = File(exists=True,

    71	                           desc='svg of roi-roi fisher z transformed correlation matrix')

    74	class AtlasConnectivity(NilearnBaseInterface, SimpleInterface):
    75	    """Calculates correlations between regions of interest"""
    76	
    77	    input_spec = AtlasConnectivityInputSpec
    78	    output_spec = AtlasConnectivityOutputSpec
    79	
    80	    def _run_interface(self, runtime):
    81	        from nilearn.input_data import NiftiLabelsMasker
    82	        from nilearn.connectome import ConnectivityMeasure
    83	        from sklearn.covariance import EmpiricalCovariance
    84	        import numpy as np
    85	        import pandas as pd
    86	        import os
    87	        import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    88	        from mne.viz import plot_connectivity_circle
    89	        import re
    90	
    91	        plt.switch_backend('Agg')
    92	
    93	        # extract timeseries from every label
    94	        masker = NiftiLabelsMasker(labels_img=self.inputs.atlas_file,
    95	                                   standardize=True, verbose=1)
    96	        timeseries = masker.fit_transform(self.inputs.timeseries_file)
    97	        # create correlation matrix
    98	        correlation_measure = ConnectivityMeasure(cov_estimator=EmpiricalCovariance(),
    99	                                                  kind="correlation")
   100	        correlation_matrix = correlation_measure.fit_transform([timeseries])[0]
   101	        np.fill_diagonal(correlation_matrix, np.NaN)
   102	
   103	        # add the atlas labels to the matrix
   104	        atlas_lut_df = pd.read_csv(self.inputs.atlas_lut, sep='\t')
   105	        regions = atlas_lut_df['regions'].values
   106	        correlation_matrix_df = pd.DataFrame(correlation_matrix, index=regions, columns=regions)
   107	
   108	        # do a fisher's r -> z transform
   109	        fisher_z_matrix_df = correlation_matrix_df.apply(_fisher_r_to_z)
   110	
   111	        # write out the file.
   112	        trial_regex = re.compile(r'.*desc-(?P<trial>[A-Za-z0-9]+)')
   113	        title = re.search(trial_regex, self.inputs.timeseries_file).groupdict()['trial']
   114	        template_name = 'desc-{trial}_correlation.{ext}'
   115	        corr_mat_fname = template_name.format(trial=title, ext="tsv")
   116	        corr_mat_path = os.path.join(runtime.cwd, corr_mat_fname)
   117	        fisher_z_matrix_df.to_csv(corr_mat_path, sep='\t', na_rep='n/a')
   118	
   119	        # save the filename in the outputs
   120	        self._results['correlation_matrix'] = corr_mat_path
   121	
   122	        # visualizations with mne
   123	        connmat = fisher_z_matrix_df.values
   124	        labels = list(fisher_z_matrix_df.index)
   125	
   126	        # plot a circle visualization of the correlation matrix
   127	        viz_mat_fname = template_name.format(trial=title, ext="svg")
   128	        viz_mat_path = os.path.join(runtime.cwd, viz_mat_fname)
   129	
   130	        n_lines = int(np.sum(connmat > 0) / 2)
   131	        fig = plt.figure(figsize=(5, 5))
   132	
   133	        plot_connectivity_circle(connmat, labels, n_lines=n_lines, fig=fig, title=title,
   134	                                 fontsize_title=10, facecolor='white', textcolor='black',
   135	                                 colormap='jet', colorbar=1, node_colors=['black'],
   136	                                 node_edgecolor=['white'], show=False, interactive=False)
   137	
   138	        fig.savefig(viz_mat_path, dpi=300)
   139	        self._results['correlation_fig'] = viz_mat_path
   140	
   141	        return runtime
   142	
   143	
   144	def _fisher_r_to_z(x):
   145	    import numpy as np
   146	    # correct any rounding errors
   147	    # correlations cannot be greater than 1.
   148	    x = np.clip(x, -1, 1)
   149	
   150	    return np.arctanh(x)
   151	
   152	
   153	def is_outlier(points, thresh=3.5):
   154	    """
   155	    Returns a boolean array with True if points are outliers and False
   156	    otherwise.
   157	
   158	    modified from nipype:
   159	    https://github.com/nipy/nipype/blob/b62d80/nipype/algorithms/confounds.py#L1129
   160	
   161	    Parameters
   162	    ----------
   163	    points: nparray
   164	        an numobservations by numdimensions numpy array of observations
   165	    thresh: float
   166	        the modified z-score to use as a threshold. Observations with
   167	        a modified z-score (based on the median absolute deviation) greater
   168	        than this value will be classified as outliers.
   169	
   170	    Returns
   171	    -------
   172	        A bolean mask, of size numobservations-length array.
   173	
   174	    .. note:: References
   175	        Boris Iglewicz and David Hoaglin (1993), "Volume 16: How to Detect and
   176	        Handle Outliers", The ASQC Basic References in Quality Control:
   177	        Statistical Techniques, Edward F. Mykytka, Ph.D., Editor.
   178	    """
   179	    import numpy as np
   180	
   181	    if len(points.shape) == 1:
   182	        points = points[:, None]
   183	    median = np.median(points, axis=0)
   184	    diff = np.sum((points - median)**2, axis=-1)
   185	    diff = np.sqrt(diff)
   186	    med_abs_deviation = np.median(diff)
   187	
   188	    modified_z_score = 0.6745 * diff / med_abs_deviation
   189	
   190	    return modified_z_score > thresh
   191

## Phase 4. TEST CREATION: Creating Reproduction Script

Let me first create a test script to reproduce the current behavior and then test our solution:<tool_call>str_replace_editor<arg_key>path</arg_key><arg_value>/workspace/HBClab__NiBetaSeries__0.4/test_nibabel_objects.py</arg_value><arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>create</arg_value><arg_key>file_text</arg_key><arg_value>#!/usr/bin/env python
"""
Test script to reproduce the issue and verify the fix for allowing 
nibabel objects to be passed to nistats interfaces.
"""

import os
import tempfile
import json
import numpy as np
import nibabel as nib
import pandas as pd

def create_test_data():
    """Create test data similar to the test fixtures."""
    # Create a temporary directory
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    
    # Create bold image data
    tr = 2.0
    tp = 200
    ix = np.arange(tp)
    
    # create voxel timeseries
    task_onsets = np.zeros(tp)
    # add activations at every 40 time points
    task_onsets[0::40] = 1
    task_onsets[3::40] = 1.5
    task_onsets[6::40] = 2
    
    from nistats.hemodynamic_models import spm_hrf
    signal = np.convolve(task_onsets, spm_hrf(tr))[0:len(task_onsets)]
    
    # csf and white matter noise
    csf = np.cos(2*np.pi*ix*(50/tp)) * 0.1
    wm = np.sin(2*np.pi*ix*(22/tp)) * 0.1
    
    # voxel time series (signal and noise)
    voxel_ts = signal + csf + wm
    
    # a 4d matrix with 2 identical timeseries
    img_data = np.array([[[voxel_ts, voxel_ts]]])
    
    # make a nifti image
    bold_img = nib.Nifti1Image(img_data, np.eye(4))
    
    # Save to file for traditional approach
    bold_file = os.path.join(temp_dir, 'bold.nii.gz')
    bold_img.to_filename(bold_file)
    
    # Create metadata
    metadata_file = os.path.join(temp_dir, 'bold.json')
    bold_metadata = {"RepetitionTime": tr, "TaskName": "test"}
    with open(metadata_file, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(bold_metadata, f)
    
    # Create brain mask
    mask_data = np.array([[[1, 1]]], dtype=np.int16)
    mask_img = nib.Nifti1Image(mask_data, np.eye(4))
    mask_file = os.path.join(temp_dir, 'mask.nii.gz')
    mask_img.to_filename(mask_file)
    
    # Create events file
    num_trials = np.where(task_onsets == 1)[0].shape[0]
    onsets = np.multiply(np.where(task_onsets == 1), tr).reshape(num_trials)
    durations = [1] * num_trials
    trial_types = ['cond1', 'cond2', 'cond3'] * (num_trials // 3)
    
    events_df = pd.DataFrame({
        'onset': onsets,
        'duration': durations,
        'trial_type': trial_types
    })
    
    events_file = os.path.join(temp_dir, 'events.tsv')
    events_df.to_csv(events_file, index=False, sep='\t')
    
    # Create confounds file
    confounds_dict = {
        'csf': np.cos(2*np.pi*ix*(50/tp)) * 0.1,
        'white_matter': np.sin(2*np.pi*ix*(22/tp)) * 0.1,
    }
    confounds_df = pd.DataFrame(confounds_dict)
    confounds_file = os.path.join(temp_dir, 'confounds.tsv')
    confounds_df.to_csv(confounds_file, index=False, sep='\t', na_rep='n/a')
    
    return {
        'temp_dir': temp_dir,
        'bold_file': bold_file,
        'bold_img': bold_img,
        'metadata_file': metadata_file,
        'mask_file': mask_file,
        'mask_img': mask_img,
        'events_file': events_file,
        'confounds_file': confounds_file,
    }

def test_current_file_based_approach(test_data):
    """Test the current file-based approach."""
    print("Testing current file-based approach...")
    
    from src.nibetaseries.interfaces.nistats import LSSBetaSeries
    
    with open(test_data['metadata_file'], 'r') as f:
        bold_metadata = json.load(f)
    
    # Traditional approach: using file paths
    beta_series = LSSBetaSeries(
        bold_file=test_data['bold_file'],
        bold_metadata=bold_metadata,
        mask_file=test_data['mask_file'],
        events_file=test_data['events_file'],
        confounds_file=test_data['confounds_file'],
        selected_confounds=['white_matter', 'csf'],
        hrf_model='spm',
        smoothing_kernel=None,
        high_pass=0.008
    )
    
    try:
        result = beta_series.run()
        print("✓ File-based approach works")
        # Clean up output files
        for beta_map in result.outputs.beta_maps:
            if os.path.exists(beta_map):
                os.remove(beta_map)
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"✗ File-based approach failed: {e}")
        return False

def test_nibabel_object_approach(test_data):
    """Test the desired nibabel object approach (should fail initially)."""
    print("Testing desired nibabel object approach...")
    
    from src.nibetaseries.interfaces.nistats import LSSBetaSeries
    
    with open(test_data['metadata_file'], 'r') as f:
        bold_metadata = json.load(f)
    
    # Desired approach: using nibabel objects directly
    # This should work after our fix
    try:
        beta_series = LSSBetaSeries(
            bold_file=test_data['bold_img'],  # Pass nibabel object instead of file path
            bold_metadata=bold_metadata,
            mask_file=test_data['mask_img'],  # Pass nibabel object instead of file path
            events_file=test_data['events_file'],
            confounds_file=test_data['confounds_file'],
            selected_confounds=['white_matter', 'csf'],
            hrf_model='spm',
            smoothing_kernel=None,
            high_pass=0.008
        )
        
        result = beta_series.run()
        print("✓ Nibabel object approach works!")
        
        # Clean up output files
        for beta_map in result.outputs.beta_maps:
            if os.path.exists(beta_map):
                os.remove(beta_map)
        return True
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"✗ Nibabel object approach failed (expected before fix): {e}")
        return False

def main():
    """Main test function."""
    print("Creating test data...")
    test_data = create_test_data()
    
    try:
        # Test current approach
        file_works = test_current_file_based_approach(test_data)
        
        # Test desired approach
        nibabel_works = test_nibabel_object_approach(test_data)
        
        print("\n--- RESULTS ---")
        print(f"File-based approach: {'PASS' if file_works else 'FAIL'}")
        print(f"Nibabel object approach: {'PASS' if nibabel_works else 'FAIL'}")
        
        if file_works and not nibabel_works:
            print("\nThis confirms the issue exists - file paths work but nibabel objects don't.")
        elif file_works and nibabel_works:
            print("\nBoth approaches work - the fix is successful!")
        else:
            print("\nUnexpected results - need to investigate.")
            
    finally:
        # Clean up
        import shutil
        shutil.rmtree(test_data['temp_dir'])

if __name__ == '__main__':

## Phase 5. FIX ANALYSIS: Clear Problem Statement and Solution<tool_call>think<arg_key>thought</arg_key><arg_value>Now I have a clear understanding of the problem and how to fix it:

**Problem Statement:**
1. Currently, LSSBetaSeries and LSABetaSeries interfaces require file paths for `bold_file` and `mask_file` inputs
2. These file paths are passed to nistats.FirstLevelModel.fit() which reads them from disk
3. Users want to pass nibabel objects directly to avoid the file I/O bottleneck
4. nistats already supports Niimg-like objects (including nibabel images) directly

**Where the problem is located:**
- In `/workspace/HBClab__NiBetaSeries__0.4/src/nibetaseries/interfaces/nistats.py`
- Lines 17 and 126: `bold_file = File(exists=True, mandatory=True, ...)`
- Lines 21 and 130: `mask_file = File(exists=True, mandatory=True, ...)`
- Lines 67 and 174: `mask=self.inputs.mask_file` (should be `mask_img=` for modern nistats)

**How the test reproduces the issue:**
- The test tries to pass nibabel.Nifti1Image objects instead of file paths
- It fails with a trait validation error saying the input must be a pathlike object or string

**Best practices for the fix:**
1. Maintain backward compatibility - file paths should still work
2. Accept both file paths and nibabel objects seamlessly
3. Use nipype's traits.Either or traits.Any for flexible input types
4. Handle the conversion internally in the _run_interface method
5. Fix the deprecated `mask` parameter to use `mask_img`

**How to fix the problem:**
1. Change the trait definitions from `File(exists=True)` to `traits.Either(File(exists=True), traits.Any())`
2. Modify the _run_interface methods to handle both file paths and nibabel objects
3. For file paths: continue as before (load files internally)
4. For nibabel objects: pass them directly to nistats
5. Update the mask parameter from `mask=` to `mask_img=` in FirstLevelModel instantiation
6. Apply the same changes to both LSSBetaSeries and LSABetaSeries classes

This approach will:
- Allow users to pass nibabel objects directly (solving the performance issue)
- Maintain full backward compatibility (existing code continues to work)
- Fix the deprecation warning about the mask parameter
- Be minimal and focused changes</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>Your thought has been logged.</tool_response><|assistant|></think>## Phase 6. FIX IMPLEMENTATION: Implementing the Solution

Let me update the LSSBetaSeries `_run_interface` method:<tool_call>str_replace_editor<arg_key>path</arg_key><arg_value>/workspace/HBClab__NiBetaSeries__0.4/src/nibetaseries/interfaces/nistats.py</arg_value><arg_key>new_str</arg_key><arg_value>    def _run_interface(self, runtime):
        from nistats import first_level_model
        import nibabel as nib
        import os

        # get t_r from bold_metadata
        t_r = self.inputs.bold_metadata['RepetitionTime']

        # get the confounds:
        if self.inputs.confounds_file and self.inputs.selected_confounds:
            confounds = _select_confounds(self.inputs.confounds_file,
                                          self.inputs.selected_confounds)
        else:
            confounds = None

        # Handle bold_file input (can be file path or nibabel object)
        bold_img = self.inputs.bold_file
        if isinstance(bold_img, str):
            # It's a file path, use as-is (backward compatibility)
            pass
        # Otherwise, it's already a nibabel object, use directly

        # Handle mask_file input (can be file path or nibabel object)
        mask_img = self.inputs.mask_file
        if isinstance(mask_img, str):
            # It's a file path, use as-is (backward compatibility)
            pass
        # Otherwise, it's already a nibabel object, use directly

        # setup the model (using mask_img instead of deprecated mask)
        model = first_level_model.FirstLevelModel(
            t_r=t_r,
            slice_time_ref=0,
            hrf_model=self.inputs.hrf_model,
            mask_img=mask_img,  # Use mask_img instead of mask
            smoothing_fwhm=self.inputs.smoothing_kernel,
            standardize=True,
            signal_scaling=0,
            high_pass=self.inputs.high_pass,
            drift_model='cosine',
            verbose=1,
            fir_delays=self.inputs.fir_delays,
        )

        # initialize dictionary to contain trial estimates (betas)
        beta_maps = {}
        design_matrix_collector = {}
        for target_trial_df, trial_type, trial_idx in \
                _lss_events_iterator(self.inputs.events_file):

            # fit the model for the target trial
            model.fit(bold_img,  # Can be file path or nibabel object
                      events=target_trial_df,
                      confounds=confounds)

            if self.inputs.hrf_model == 'fir':
                # FS modeling
                for delay in self.inputs.fir_delays:
                    delay_ttype = trial_type+'_delay_{}'.format(delay)
                    new_delay_ttype = delay_ttype.replace('_delay_{}'.format(delay),
                                                          'Delay{}Vol'.format(delay))
                    beta_map = model.compute_contrast(
                        delay_ttype, output_type='effect_size')
                    if new_delay_ttype in beta_maps:
                        beta_maps[new_delay_ttype].append(beta_map)
                    else:
                        beta_maps[new_delay_ttype] = [beta_map]
            else:
                # calculate the beta map
                beta_map = model.compute_contrast(trial_type, output_type='effect_size')
                design_matrix_collector[trial_idx] = model.design_matrices_[0]
                # assign beta map to appropriate list
                if trial_type in beta_maps:
                    beta_maps[trial_type].append(beta_map)
                else:
                    beta_maps[trial_type] = [beta_map]

        # make a beta series from each beta map list
        beta_series_template = os.path.join(runtime.cwd,
                                            'desc-{trial_type}_betaseries.nii.gz')
        # collector for the betaseries files
        beta_series_lst = []
        for t_type, betas in beta_maps.items():
            beta_series = nib.funcs.concat_images(betas)
            nib.save(beta_series, beta_series_template.format(trial_type=t_type))
            beta_series_lst.append(beta_series_template.format(trial_type=t_type))

        self._results['beta_maps'] = beta_series_lst
        self._results['design_matrices'] = design_matrix_collector
        return runtime</arg_value><arg_key>old_str</arg_key><arg_value>    def _run_interface(self, runtime):
        from nistats import first_level_model
        import nibabel as nib
        import os

        # get t_r from bold_metadata
        t_r = self.inputs.bold_metadata['RepetitionTime']

        # get the confounds:
        if self.inputs.confounds_file and self.inputs.selected_confounds:
            confounds = _select_confounds(self.inputs.confounds_file,
                                          self.inputs.selected_confounds)
        else:
            confounds = None

        # setup the model
        model = first_level_model.FirstLevelModel(
            t_r=t_r,
            slice_time_ref=0,
            hrf_model=self.inputs.hrf_model,
            mask=self.inputs.mask_file,
            smoothing_fwhm=self.inputs.smoothing_kernel,
            standardize=True,
            signal_scaling=0,
            high_pass=self.inputs.high_pass,
            drift_model='cosine',
            verbose=1,
            fir_delays=self.inputs.fir_delays,
        )

        # initialize dictionary to contain trial estimates (betas)
        beta_maps = {}
        design_matrix_collector = {}
        for target_trial_df, trial_type, trial_idx in \
                _lss_events_iterator(self.inputs.events_file):

            model.fit(self.inputs.bold_file,
                      events=target_trial_df,
                      confounds=confounds)

                                                          'Delay{}Vol'.format(delay))

                        delay_ttype, output_type='effect_size')
                    if new_delay_ttype in beta_maps:
                        beta_maps[new_delay_ttype].append(beta_map)
                    else:
                        beta_maps[new_delay_ttype] = [beta_map]
            else:
                # calculate the beta map
                beta_map = model.compute_contrast(trial_type, output_type='effect_size')
                design_matrix_collector[trial_idx] = model.design_matrices_[0]
                # assign beta map to appropriate list
                if trial_type in beta_maps:
                    beta_maps[trial_type].append(beta_map)
                else:
                    beta_maps[trial_type] = [beta_map]

        # make a beta series from each beta map list
        beta_series_template = os.path.join(runtime.cwd,
                                            'desc-{trial_type}_betaseries.nii.gz')
        # collector for the betaseries files
        beta_series_lst = []
        for t_type, betas in beta_maps.items():
            beta_series = nib.funcs.concat_images(betas)
            nib.save(beta_series, beta_series_template.format(trial_type=t_type))
            beta_series_lst.append(beta_series_template.format(trial_type=t_type))

        self._results['beta_maps'] = beta_series_lst
        self._results['design_matrices'] = design_matrix_collector
        return runtime</arg_value><arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>str_replace</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>The file /workspace/HBClab__NiBetaSeries__0.4/src/nibetaseries/interfaces/nistats.py has been edited.         # get t_r from bold_metadata

        # get the confounds:
        if self.inputs.confounds_file and self.inputs.selected_confounds:
            confounds = _select_confounds(self.inputs.confounds_file,
                                          self.inputs.selected_confounds)
        else:
            confounds = None

        # Handle bold_file input (can be file path or nibabel object)
        bold_img = self.inputs.bold_file

            # It's a file path, use as-is (backward compatibility)
            pass
        # Otherwise, it's already a nibabel object, use directly

            # It's a file path, use as-is (backward compatibility)
            pass

            t_r=t_r,

            standardize=True,

            high_pass=self.inputs.high_pass,
            drift_model='cosine',
            verbose=1
        )

        beta_maps = {}

        design_matrix = model.design_matrices_[0]

            t_type = lsa_df.loc[i_trial, 'original_trial_type']

            # assign beta map to appropriate list

        from nistats import first_level_model
        import nibabel as nib
        import os

        # get t_r from bold_metadata
        t_r = self.inputs.bold_metadata['RepetitionTime']

        # get the confounds:
        if self.inputs.confounds_file and self.inputs.selected_confounds:
            confounds = _select_confounds(self.inputs.confounds_file,
                                          self.inputs.selected_confounds)
        else:
            confounds = None

        # setup the model
        model = first_level_model.FirstLevelModel(
            t_r=t_r,
            slice_time_ref=0,
            hrf_model=self.inputs.hrf_model,
            mask=self.inputs.mask_file,
            smoothing_fwhm=self.inputs.smoothing_kernel,
            standardize=True,
            signal_scaling=0,
            high_pass=self.inputs.high_pass,
            drift_model='cosine',
            verbose=1
        )

        # initialize dictionary to contain trial estimates (betas)
        beta_maps = {}
        lsa_df = _lsa_events_converter(self.inputs.events_file)
        model.fit(self.inputs.bold_file, events=lsa_df, confounds=confounds)
        design_matrix = model.design_matrices_[0]
        for i_trial in lsa_df.index:
            t_name = lsa_df.loc[i_trial, 'trial_type']
            t_type = lsa_df.loc[i_trial, 'original_trial_type']

            # calculate the beta map
            beta_map = model.compute_contrast(t_name, output_type='effect_size')

            # assign beta map to appropriate list
            if t_type in beta_maps:
                beta_maps[t_type].append(beta_map)
            else:
                beta_maps[t_type] = [beta_map]

        # make a beta series from each beta map list
        beta_series_template = os.path.join(runtime.cwd,
                                            'desc-{trial_type}_betaseries.nii.gz')
        # collector for the betaseries files
        beta_series_lst = []
        for t_type, betas in beta_maps.items():
            beta_series = nib.funcs.concat_images(betas)
            nib.save(beta_series, beta_series_template.format(trial_type=t_type))
            beta_series_lst.append(beta_series_template.format(trial_type=t_type))

        self._results['beta_maps'] = beta_series_lst
        self._results['design_matrices'] = [design_matrix]
        return runtime</arg_value><arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>str_replace</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>The file /workspace/HBClab__NiBetaSeries__0.4/src/nibetaseries/interfaces/nistats.py has been edited. def create_simple_test():
    """Create minimal test data."""
    # Create a temporary directory
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    
    # Create simple bold image data
    data = np.random.rand(5, 5, 5, 10)
    bold_img = nib.Nifti1Image(data, np.eye(4))
    
    # Save to file 
    bold_file = os.path.join(temp_dir, 'bold.nii.gz')
    bold_img.to_filename(bold_file)
    
    # Create simple mask
    mask_data = np.ones((5, 5, 5))
    mask_img = nib.Nifti1Image(mask_data, np.eye(4))
    mask_file = os.path.join(temp_dir, 'mask.nii.gz')
    mask_img.to_filename(mask_file)
    
    return {
        'temp_dir': temp_dir,
        'bold_file': bold_file,
        'bold_img': bold_img,
        'mask_file': mask_file,
        'mask_img': mask_img,
    }

"""
Comprehensive test to verify that both LSS and LSA interfaces 
work with nibabel objects.
"""

import os
import tempfile
import json
import numpy as np
import nibabel as nib
import pandas as pd

def create_test_data():
    """Create test data."""
    # Create a temporary directory
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    
    # Create bold image data
    tr = 2.0
    tp = 50  # Smaller for faster testing
    ix = np.arange(tp)
    
    # create voxel timeseries
    task_onsets = np.zeros(tp)
    task_onsets[0::10] = 1
    task_onsets[3::10] = 1.5
    task_onsets[6::10] = 2
    
    from nistats.hemodynamic_models import spm_hrf
    signal = np.convolve(task_onsets, spm_hrf(tr))[0:len(task_onsets)]
    
    # csf and white matter noise
    csf = np.cos(2*np.pi*ix*(50/tp)) * 0.1
    wm = np.sin(2*np.pi*ix*(22/tp)) * 0.1
    
    # voxel time series (signal and noise)
    voxel_ts = signal + csf + wm
    
    # a 4d matrix with 2 identical timeseries
    img_data = np.array([[[voxel_ts, voxel_ts]]])
    
    # make a nifti image
    bold_img = nib.Nifti1Image(img_data, np.eye(4))
    
    # Save to file for traditional approach
    bold_file = os.path.join(temp_dir, 'bold.nii.gz')
    bold_img.to_filename(bold_file)
    
    # Create metadata
    metadata_file = os.path.join(temp_dir, 'bold.json')
    bold_metadata = {"RepetitionTime": tr, "TaskName": "test"}
    with open(metadata_file, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(bold_metadata, f)
    
    # Create brain mask
    mask_data = np.array([[[1, 1]]], dtype=np.int16)
    mask_img = nib.Nifti1Image(mask_data, np.eye(4))
    mask_file = os.path.join(temp_dir, 'mask.nii.gz')
    mask_img.to_filename(mask_file)
    
    # Create events file
    onset_indices = np.where(task_onsets == 1)[0]
    num_trials = len(onset_indices)
    onsets = onset_indices * tr
    durations = [1] * num_trials
    # Repeat the trial types enough times to cover all trials
    trial_types_base = ['cond1', 'cond2', 'cond3']
    trial_types = (trial_types_base * ((num_trials // len(trial_types_base)) + 1))[:num_trials]
    
    events_df = pd.DataFrame({
        'onset': onsets,
        'duration': durations,
        'trial_type': trial_types
    })
    
    events_file = os.path.join(temp_dir, 'events.tsv')
    events_df.to_csv(events_file, index=False, sep='\t')
    
    # Create confounds file
    confounds_dict = {
        'csf': np.cos(2*np.pi*ix*(50/tp)) * 0.1,
        'white_matter': np.sin(2*np.pi*ix*(22/tp)) * 0.1,
    }
    confounds_df = pd.DataFrame(confounds_dict)
    confounds_file = os.path.join(temp_dir, 'confounds.tsv')
    confounds_df.to_csv(confounds_file, index=False, sep='\t', na_rep='n/a')
    
    return {
        'temp_dir': temp_dir,
        'bold_file': bold_file,
        'bold_img': bold_img,
        'metadata_file': metadata_file,
        'mask_file': mask_file,
        'mask_img': mask_img,
        'events_file': events_file,
        'confounds_file': confounds_file,
    }

def test_lss_with_files(test_data):
    """Test LSS with file paths."""
    print("Testing LSS with file paths...")
    
    from src.nibetaseries.interfaces.nistats import LSSBetaSeries
    
    with open(test_data['metadata_file'], 'r') as f:
        bold_metadata = json.load(f)
    
    beta_series = LSSBetaSeries(
        bold_file=test_data['bold_file'],
        bold_metadata=bold_metadata,
        mask_file=test_data['mask_file'],
        events_file=test_data['events_file'],
        confounds_file=test_data['confounds_file'],
        selected_confounds=['white_matter', 'csf'],
        hrf_model='spm',
        smoothing_kernel=None,
        high_pass=0.008
    )
    
    try:
        result = beta_series.run()
        # Clean up output files
        for beta_map in result.outputs.beta_maps:
            if os.path.exists(beta_map):
                os.remove(beta_map)
        print("✓ LSS with file paths works")
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"✗ LSS with file paths failed: {e}")
        return False

def test_lss_with_nibabel(test_data):

        selected_confounds=['white_matter', 'csf'],
        hrf_model='spm',
        smoothing_kernel=None,
        high_pass=0.008

def main():
    """Run comprehensive tests."""
    print("Creating test data...")
    test_data = create_test_data()
    
    try:
        results = []
        
        # Test all combinations
        results.append(("LSS File Paths", test_lss_with_files(test_data)))
        results.append(("LSS Nibabel Objects", test_lss_with_nibabel(test_data)))
        results.append(("LSA File Paths", test_lsa_with_files(test_data)))
        results.append(("LSA Nibabel Objects", test_lsa_with_nibabel(test_data)))
        
        print("\n--- COMPREHENSIVE TEST RESULTS ---")
        all_passed = True
        for test_name, passed in results:
            status = "PASS" if passed else "FAIL"
            print(f"{test_name}: {status}")
            if not passed:
                all_passed = False
                
        if all_passed:
            print("\n🎉 All tests passed! The feature is working correctly.")
        else:
            print("\n❌ Some tests failed.")
            
    finally:
        # Clean up
        import shutil
        shutil.rmtree(test_data['temp_dir'])
